rArr[i];\n        const double q = qArr[i];\n        const double T = tArr[i];\n        const double sigma = volArr[i];\n\n        if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n            out[i] = 0.0;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        const double sqrtT = sqrt(T);\n        const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrtT);\n        const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrtT;\n\n        const double e1 = erf(d1 * M_SQRT1_2);\n        const double e2 = erf(d2 * M_SQRT1_2);\n        const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n        const double carry = exp(-q * T);\n\n        double price;\n        if (type[i] == CALL) {\n            price = S * carry * (0.5 * (1.0 + e1)) - K * discount * (0.5 * (1.0 + e2));\n        } else { // PUT\n            price = K * discount * for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n {\n\nconstexpr int kBlockSize = 256;\nconstexpr int kStreamsPerDevice = 4;\n// Chunk of options moved/priced per stream iteration. off < end; off += ctx.chunk, s = (s + 1) % kStreamsPerDevice) {\n        const size_t count = std::min(ctx.chunk, end - off);\n        cudaStream_t stream = ctx.streams[s];\n\n        const double* const hostFields[6] = {options.strike + off, options.spot + off,\n                                             options.q + off,      options.r + off,\n                                             options.t + off,      options.vol + off};\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(ctx.dType[s], options.type + off, count * sizeof(int),\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n        for (int f = 0; Only the six fields the formula uses (+ type) are moved: 52 B/option instead of the 72 B `OptionInput`.\n- **Multi-GPU**: the option range is split into contiguous per-device slices, one host thread per device, so all four PCIe links are used in parallel. Small inputs stay on a single device (and skip thread spawning) where orchestration would dominate.\n- **Pipelined**: 4 streams per device, each chunk doing H2D → kernel → D2H, so copies overlap compute and each other. Chunk size adapts to the slice (64 Ki … 1 Mi options) — small chunks win at ~1 M options, large ones at ~100 M.\n- Context creation, allocations and a copy/compute warm-up (which also brings the GPUs off idle clocks) happen **before** the timer; the measured region is exactly the pricing work, transfers included.\n- Option generation is multithreaded (it was untimed before and still is). `validateResults` keeps its signature but is handed just the prefix it inspects, avoiding a second multi-GB AoS copy of the inputs.\n\n**`black-scholes/CMakeLists.txt`** — enables the CUDA language, compiles `black_scholes.cpp` as CUDA (`set_source_files_properties`), `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`, keeps `-O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra` on the host side. Executable name unchanged; no new files or dependencies.\n\n## Results (4× RTX 3090, options/s = timed region incl. PCIe transfers)\n\n| N | serial | CUDA | speedup |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 M | 90.3 ms | 2.13 ms | 42× |\n| 10 M | 898 ms | 12.2 ms | 74× |\n| 100 M | 9141 ms | 108 ms | **85×** |\n\nScaling across GPUs at 100 M: 1 GPU 510 ms → 2 GPUs 222 ms → 4 GPUs 108 ms.\n\n## Correctness\nCompared against a rebuild of the original serial code: sum/min/max/samples agree to **≤1.2e-15 relative** across N = 1, 7, 1000, 100003, 5 M. The results are not bit-identical, and can't be — device `erf`/`log`/`exp` differ from glibc by <1 ULP (`-fmad=false` made it worse, not better, so FMA contraction is left on). The printed `Hash:` field is a bitwise FNV of the doubles, so it will differ from a serial reference; the numeric fields are the meaningful comparison. Validation passes, `-h`/unknown-option/`-n 0` paths behave as before (with `-n 0`, \"Options per second\" prints `0` rather than `-nan`, since the timed region is no longer exactly 0 µs)."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":502,"cache_read_input_tokens":62264,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":502},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2f03f3a4-b4b7-490a-bf9a-c15b5f67bd35","uuid":"fc07b509-cdcb-4768-9bca-db5f11a66bdb","timestamp":"2026-09-07T03:45:12.460Z","request_id":"req_011CeoPcvF9N17JKyS5dHKZt"}
